(b)

Distributed Protocol Specification, Compatibility through Projection

Figure 4
.
Inter-Component Communication and
Compatibility via Projection

3.
Hybrid Types for Protocol Specification

3.1.
Background: Preliminaries of Multiparty Session Types

We give a short summary of
multiparty session types

(
Honda et al. 2016
;
Scalas et al. 2019
;
Coppo et al. 2015
;
Yoshida and Gheri 2020
)
;
specifically, our theory
is based on the formulation
in
(
Deniélou and Yoshida 2013
)
,
extended to parallel composition of global types.
The notation for our MPST system is standard
(directly adapted from
(
Castro-Perez et al. 2021
)
).

Atoms of our syntax are: a set of
roles
(or
participants
), ranged over by
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}},\dots
, a set of
(type) variables
, ranged over by
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{Y}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots
; and a set of labels, ranged over by
{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{0}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{1}}}},\dots,{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}},\dots
.

Definition 3.1 (Sorts, Global Types, and Local Types).

Sorts
,

global types
, and

local types
, ranged over by
S
,
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

respectively, are inductive datatypes generated by:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}::={\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small unit}}}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small int}}}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small bool}}}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}\texttt{\small+}{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}}}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}\texttt{\small*}{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}}}\hskip 18.49988pt\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}::=\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}.\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}\,|\,\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\\
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}::=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}.\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\end{array}

where,
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}
,
I\neq\emptyset
, and

{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}
when
i\neq j,
for all
i,j\in I
, in
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

The
global message

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
describes a protocol where participant
p
sends to
q
one message with label
{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}
and a value of sort
{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}
as payload, for
some
i\in I
; then, depending on which
{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}
was sent by
p
, the protocol continues as
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}
. The type
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
represents a
terminated protocol
.
Recursive protocol
is modelled as
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mu}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}.\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, where recursion
variable

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is bound.
The
parallel

construct
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}\,|\,\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
describes a protocol composed
by two independent ones.
The participants of
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

are required to be disjoint:
no communication happens between

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
but only internally in each one of them
(for a broader discussion, see §
5.1
).
The intuition for local types
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}.\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

is the same as for global types.
The
send type

\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
says that the participant implementing the type must choose a labelled message to send to
q
; if the participant chooses the label
{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}
, it must include in the message to
q
a payload value of sort

{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}
, and continue as prescribed by
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. The
receive
type

\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
requires to wait to receive a value of sort
{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}
(for some
i\in I
) from the participant
p
, via a message with label
{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}
; then the process continues as prescribed by
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

We are interested in types that are (1)

guarded
—e.g.,

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mu}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}.\allowbreak\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is a valid
global type, whereas
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mu}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{.}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is not—(detail in Appendix
B.1
, Definition
B.1
)
and (2)

closed
, i.e., all variables are bound by
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mu}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. In messages, sends and receives, the payload type can be omitted (e.g.,
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}.\dots}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
), when only a label is exchanged. We assume that global and local types are always guarded,
and that, in
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}\,|\,\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
are closed.

Projection
plays a central role in MPST theories: it connects the protocol discipline, provided by the global type, with the local types that separately describe the behaviour of each participant.

Definition 3.2 (Projection for Global Types).

The
projection of a global type onto a role
r
is a partial function defined by recursion on
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, whenever the recursive call is defined:

\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-end]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-var]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{X}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-rec]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt{(\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}.\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}.}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}})\ \text{if}\ \color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}.}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}})\ \text{is guarded, else, if}\ (\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}.\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\ \text{is closed,}\ {(\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{X}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}.\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mathtt{end}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-par]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}\,|\,\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}\ \text{if}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\ \text{is a participant of}\ \color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{,}\ \color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{otherwise}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-send]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i})}.({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{i}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}={{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-recv]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i})}.({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{i}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}={{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-merge]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{i}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}})\ \text{if}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\ \text{and}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\end{array}

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}
is undefined if none of the above applies.
Merging
(
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
) is defined as a partial operator over two local types such that:
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
for every type, it delves inductively inside all constructors (e.g.,
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}^{\prime}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i})}.(\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}^{\prime}_{i})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
), and

\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}^{\prime}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}^{\prime}_{k}\}_{k\in I\cap J}\cup\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{k}\}_{k\in I\backslash J}\cup\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}^{\prime}_{k}\}_{k\in J\backslash I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

We describe the clauses of Definition
3.2
.

[proj-end]
,
[proj-var]
, and
[proj-rec]
are standard.

[proj-send]
(resp.
[proj-recv]
) states that a global type starting with a message from
r
to
q
(resp. from
p
to
r
) projects onto a sending (resp. receiving) local type

\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{i}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}}\}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(resp.
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{i}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}}\}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
), provided that the continuations

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{i}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}{\upharpoonright}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}
are also projections of the corresponding
global-type continuations
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.
[proj-merge]
states that, if the projected global type
starts with an interaction between
p
and
q
, and if we are
projecting it onto a third participant
r
, then the projection is defined (and we can skip the message
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
) if all the continuations project onto
mergeable
types (according to the merge operator
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
defined above).

[proj-par]
states that
projecting a parallel type
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}\,|\,\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
on
r
is the same as projecting
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
or
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
onto
r
, depending on whether
r
is a participant of one or the other type.

By projecting a global type
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
onto all participants
(
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, for the generic role
r
), we obtain a collection of local types
\{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\}
,
where
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

is the behavioural type
for
r
.
Existing MPST theories (e.g.,
(
Deniélou and Yoshida 2013
)
)
guarantee that a session of well-typed
implementations of the participants
inherits semantic guarantees for its communication, from
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.
Our development in §
4
is
compatible with such
theories: result of composition is a
well-formed
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, thus, implementations of projected
local types benefit from well-established semantics
results from the literature.

3.2.
Hybrid Types

To allow the specification of interacting subprotocols,
we enrich the syntax of global types with local constructs.
We thus obtain “hybrid” types,
which use global messages for intra-protocol communication
and local sends and receives as openings
for inter-protocol communication.

Definition 3.3 (Hybrid Types).

Hybrid types
are defined inductively by:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}&::=&\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~~\mathbf{|\!\!|}~~\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\end{array}

where, in
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}
,
I\neq\emptyset,
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}
when
i\neq j,
for all
i,j\in I
. We indicate the datatype of hybrid types with the notation
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathcal{H}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

The intuition behind each construct
is the same as in
Definition

3.1
, but
we write
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

in place of
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

in place of
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.
For local types,
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(resp.
\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{L}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
) describes
the communication of the participant
p

sending a message to
q

(resp.
q
receiving a message from
p
),
and then continuing with
interactions
all involving
p
(resp.
q
) as a subject
.
Therefore, such subject can be left implicit.
For hybrid types, instead,
different (internal) subjects
interact both with internal and external participants.
For instance:

{}^{(a)}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{1}}}}.}\ \ {}^{(b)}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{2}}}}.}\ \ {}^{(c)}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{3}}}}.}\ \ {}^{(d)}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(a)

p
sends an external message to
q
;

(b)

p
exchanges a message internally with
r
;

(c)

r
receives an external message from
q
; and

(d)

the protocol terminates.

Definition 3.4 (Internal and External Participants).

We define the sets of
internal participants
and
external participants
of a hybrid type
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
by recursion:

\hskip-8.53581pt\begin{array}[]{ll}\begin{array}[]{l}{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\emptyset\hskip 9.24994pt{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\emptyset\\
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})={\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})={\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cup{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\}\cup\bigcup_{i\in I}{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cup\bigcup_{i\in I}{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cup\bigcup_{i\in I}{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\end{array}&\hskip-11.38109pt\begin{array}[]{l}\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\emptyset\hskip 9.24994pt\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\emptyset\\
\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cup\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cup\bigcup_{i\in I}\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\}\cup\bigcup_{i\in I}\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\bigcup_{i\in I}\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\end{array}\end{array}

We define, for hybrid types,
guardedness
and
closedness
,
as for global and local types (Definition
3.1
).
We require that all hybrid types in this paper
are guarded
(detail in Appendix
B.1
,
Definition
B.1
) and that, for all

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\emptyset
.
Also, we require well-formedness for parallel constructs: for

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
must be closed and

({\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cup\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}))\allowbreak\cap({\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cup\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}))=\emptyset
.
Namely, the parallel construct
describes
communication that happens independently,
within two separate groups of participants.
We express
global and local types in terms of hybrid types
, with two predicates on
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathcal{H}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
:

\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{isGlobal(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
holds iff
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is formed only by global constructs
(global type syntax in Definition
3.1
); and

\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{isLocal(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,1})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
holds iff
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})

contains at most one element (hence
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
contains only local constructs,
see local types in Definition
3.1
).

Example 3.5.

We use as a recurring example
the company from §
2
.
A designer,
D_{\sf str}
, describes the protocol
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf str}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

for the strategic team, as in Figure
2(c)
:

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf str}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}:=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ad}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{prod}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{prod}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}}!{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{prod}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{ok}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ad}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{go}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1},\\
{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{wait}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ad}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{wait}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\end{array}\right\}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

First,
d
sends internally a product ID to
ad
, then a similar external message to
s
, of the sales department, and to
{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}
, of the finance department.
d
waits in a recursive loop for
{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}
to give the
ok
. When this happens,
d
internally communicates to
ad
that they can proceed with the product advertisement.
For
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf str}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, the sets of internal and external participants are

{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf str}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ad}}}}\}
and
\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf str}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}\}
.
We observe that
D_{\sf str}
is not concerned with
the communication that happens internally
to the sales department or the financial one,
nor with the communication between these two.
Designers
D_{\sf sales}
and
D_{\sf fin}

independently give protocols
for the sales and financial departments
respectively (as in Figure
2(c)
):

\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf sales}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{prod}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{price}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{w}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{publish}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1},\\
{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{wait}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{w}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{wait}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\end{array}\right\}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf fin}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}}?{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{prod}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}\to{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}2}}:}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{prod}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}2}}\to{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}:}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{price}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{ok}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{price}}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small nat}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1},\\
{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{wait}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{wait}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\textit{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{wait}}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\end{array}\right\}}\end{array}

In the sales department, once
d
has communicated the product,
s
waits in a loop for the decision about the price from the financial department, then gives to the website administrator
w
the command to publish.
We have that
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf sales}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{w}}}}\}
and
\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf sales}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}\}
. The decision about the price of the product is taken by
{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}2}}
, and communicated internally to the financial department with
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}2}}\to{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\dots}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
; then
{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}}
communicates the decision to the other departments, which can continue with their internal communication. We have that
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf fin}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}1}},{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{f}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}2}}\}
and
\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{\sf fin}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{d}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\}
.

In §
4
,
we prove
the above types

compatible

and
compose
them into a single
global type.

3.3.
Projection and Localiser

We introduce
projection
and
localiser
for hybrid types. These operators play are fundamental for defining compatibility and, ultimately, achieving compositionality.

Definition 3.6 (Projection).

The (generalised) projection of a hybrid type on the set of participants
E
, is a partial operator,
\_\upharpoonright_{E}:\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathcal{H}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\rightarrow\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathcal{H}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, recursively defined by the following clauses (whenever the recursive call is defined):

\begin{array}[]{ll}\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-end]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\upharpoonright_{E}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-var]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\upharpoonright_{E}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\end{array}\\
\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-rec]}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if}\ \color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{is guarded,}\\
\text{else, if}\ (\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\ \text{is closed,}\ (\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\\
\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-par]}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}\\
\text{if $E\cap{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{\mathit{j}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=\emptyset$, with $i,j\in\{1,2\}$ and $i\neq j$}\end{array}\\
\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-send]}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\\
\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E$ and ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E$}\\
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E$}\end{cases}\\[8.53581pt]
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-recv]}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\\
\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E$ and ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E$}\\
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E$}\end{cases}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[proj-msg]}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\\
\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E$ and ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E$ and ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E$}\end{cases}\\[28.45274pt]
\text{and undefined otherwise.}\end{array}\end{array}

Merging
(
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
) is defined as a partial commutative operator
over two hybrid types such that: for all
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, it delves inductively inside all constructors (e.g.,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\allowbreak\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\allowbreak=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i})}.(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}{}_{i})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
), and

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{\prime}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\allowbreak\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\allowbreak\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{\prime}_{k}\}_{k\in I\cap J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\cup\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{k}\}_{k\in I\backslash J}\cup\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{\prime}_{k}\}_{k\in J\backslash I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

With respect to Definition
3.2
, we now allow projection onto a
set
of participants, and we introduce rules for projecting send and receive constructs. We highlight the differences below:

•

[proj-msg]
defines
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
to be projected onto
E
, if
both
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E
; in this case the structure of the global message
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is maintained in the projected type;

•

[proj-send]
defines projection when the sender
p
is in
E
and
q
is not, and when both
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E
;

•

[proj-recv]
defines projection when the receiver
q
is in
E
and
p
is not, and when both
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E
.

Remark 3.7.

Projection is defined only onto
sets of
internal
participants; e.g.,

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
can be projected
onto
q
, but not onto
p
;
also,
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}})\subseteq{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})
.
If we project a hybrid type onto a singleton,
we obtain a local type:

\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{isLocal(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\}}})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.
Furthermore, if
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{isGlobal(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, then

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\}}}
is exactly the traditional
MPST projection of the
global type
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
onto
p
,

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}}
(Definition
3.2
).

Definition 3.8 (Localiser).

The localiser of a hybrid type is a partial operator,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\;\_}:\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathcal{H}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\rightarrow\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathcal{H}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, recursively defined by the following clauses (whenever the recursive call is defined):

\begin{array}[]{ll}\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[loc-end]}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[loc-var]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[loc-par]}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[loc-rec]}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if}\ \color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{is guarded, else, if}\ \color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{is closed,}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[loc-send]}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[loc-recv]}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\\
\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\scriptsize\textsc{[loc-msg]}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}\\
\lx@intercol\text{and undefined otherwise.}\hfil\lx@intercol\end{array}

Merging for the localiser
(
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}
) is a partial
commutative operator over two hybrid types such that: for all
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
it delves inductively inside all constructors (e.g.,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\allowbreak\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i})}.(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}{}_{i})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
), and

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{\prime}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{\prime}_{k}\}_{k\in I\cap J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\cup\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{k}\}_{k\in I\backslash J}\cup\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}{({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k})}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{\prime}_{k}\}_{k\in J\backslash I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

The localiser is a forgetful operator that
preserves local constructs
and discards global messages.
[loc-end]
,
[loc-var]
,
[loc-rec]
, and
[loc-par]
preserve the non-message structure of the type, into its localisation.
[loc-send]
and
[loc-recv]
state that send construct (an internal participant
p
sends to an external participant
q
) and receive construct (an internal participant
q
receives from an external participant
p
) are to be maintained and their continuations
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
localised into
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.
[loc-msg]
is the central rule: each global message has to be skipped and its continuations need to be merged.

Remark 3.9.

The merge operator for the localiser,
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}
,
is
dual to the merge for projection
,
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
.
To build the intuition behind this,
let us consider the following hybrid type:

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{1}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{3}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1},}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\allowbreak\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{2}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{4}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}\}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.
First,
p
chooses on which branch to take,
by
internally
sending either

{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{1}}}}
or
{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{2}}}}
to
q
;
then according to the chosen branch,
p

itself sends a different
external
message
to
r
. When we localise the above
type we obtain

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{3}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1},{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{4}}}}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}\}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
namely we have merged send
constructs with different labels,

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{3}}}}
and

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{4}}}}
.
From the point of view of the external receiver
r
,
it makes no difference whether
such choice has been taken by
p

at the time
p
sends to
r
(with
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
),
or at a precedent stage of communication,
internal to
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
(with
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
).
This intuition is proven correct
by the results from the next section,
when we define
a compatibility notion,
based on localiser and projection,
and we prove compositionality.

4.
Compositionality for Distributed Specification

In §
3
, we have set definitions in place to compose subprotocols.
In particular,
following the overview of Figure
4(b)
, §
2
, what we need is:

•

hybrid types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{N}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
for the multiple components of the communicating system;

•

a compatibility hybrid type
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(we sometimes use the notation
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, when
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is a global type, namely when
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{isGlobal(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
)
that disciplines the inter-component communication; and

•

the property that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
projects onto the localisations of
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{N}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
(compatibility).

In this section, we present our journey to
multiparty compositional specification in three steps:

(1)

we focus on a single hybrid type
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, for which
compatibility holds:
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{i}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
;
we build a new type
\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E_{i}}\;{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}\;{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}
,
whose projection on
E_{i}
coincides with
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
and which contains the information
for external communication from
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
(Theorem
4.4
);

(2)

we show how Step 1 is a base case for
composing multiple compatible protocols:
from
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathcal{B}^{1}}
, we recursively define

\mathcal{B}\;{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}\;{([\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{N}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}])}
,
which projects onto
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

for all

i=1,\dots,N

(compositionality, Theorem
4.7
);

(3)

we prove that projection composes over
the subset relation (Theorem
4.9
);
this guarantees the applicability and correctness of our result:
if
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is a
global type
,
we obtain a well-formed global type
\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5}{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathcal{B}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;([\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{N}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}])}}

for the whole system,
the projections of which, onto every participant,
are the same as the projections of the subprotocols
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
(Corollary
4.10
).

4.1.
Step 1: Building Back a Single Subprotocol

Our first step towards compositionality
is also the most technical of the three. In this section we
present the main design choices, both in constructions
and in proofs, that make our theory sound.
For more details, we refer to Appendix
B.1
.

We are given
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, the compatibility type disciplining communication
happening among subprotocols, and with one of these subprotocols

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, describing the communication
from the point of view of its internal participants,
contained in the set
E
.
The local constructs of
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
are
compatible
with
what prescribed by
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
for communicating externally, formally:

(
C
)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

The above notion is designed for the direct
composition of multiple subprotocols:
the hybrid type
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
for one component is
checked compatible,
not against other components, but
against
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, which gives global guidance
for inter-component communication.
This design choice differentiates our theory from previous
work, where compatibility is checked by
directly matching the inputs and outputs of two
separate components
(see §
6
for further discussion).
With such compatible types, we build
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}

that retains the information about external communication of
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

and about internal communication in the component
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\\

\hskip 9.24994pt\text{with ${\sf UP}_{i}=\pi_{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}]_{i\in I}}})$, if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E$}\end{cases}\end{array}&\hskip-18.49428pt\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}=\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \\
