\hskip 18.49988pt\text{if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$, $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$, and $I=J\cup K$,}\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{or if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$, $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$, and $I=J=K$,}\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{or if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$, $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$,}\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\text{and $I=J=K$}\end{array}\\
\hskip-4.83694pt\begin{array}[]{l}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmLP}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}]_{i\in I}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}=([\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}])_{i\in I}$, in any other case }\end{array}\end{cases}\end{array}

Given a set of participants
E
, the partial function
unmerge for localiser

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}
(again we drop the dependency on
E
in the notation) is defined if
\bigcup_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\in\sf{set}(L)}{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})
by the following equations, by recursion on the list of hybrid types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, whenever the recursive call is defined:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[]}}=\text{undefined}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}=[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}=[\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}),\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})]\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))}}=\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}({\sf set}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))}})\#{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}({\sf set}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))}})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}\end{array}

We observe that
{\sf{length}}\;({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})={\sf{length}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and that
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
, where
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is the
t
-th element of the list
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}
(when defined).

Lemma B.11 (Unmerge for Localiser).

We fix a set of participants
E
, and we are given hybrid types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
t=1,\dots,n
, such that:

(a)

{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\subseteq E
, for all
t=n
,

(b)

\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E=\emptyset
,

(c)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{t=1,\dots,n}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
;

then, for all
t=1,\dots,n
, we call
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
the
t
-th element of the list
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}
and we have

(1)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}={\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and

(2)

\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
, for all
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
.

Proof.

Let us first prove the lemma for
n=2
. In particular, hypothesis
(c)
gives us
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We need to prove the thesis for
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})
, namely that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}^{\prime}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
, for all
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
. We proceed by induction on
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})+\sf{max}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}),\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))
.

Base cases
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
are trivial. Let us consider
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
; if
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and the thesis follows by definition. If
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{L}_{1}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{L}_{2}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and the thesis follows by applying induction hypothesis to
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{L}_{1}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{L}_{2}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

For case
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, we can assume without loss of generality
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
, hence
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{(\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}))|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{(\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}))|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
. We conclude by induction hypothesis.

We are left with the message cases
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. These are all analogous and we will see in detail only
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, which is the most general as it can project on every message construct:
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
.

[
\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\subseteq E
] then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and, since
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}
cannot output a global message
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
, by hypothesis
(c)
the thesis holds vacuously.

[
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E
] We have (hypothesis
(c)
)
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, namely

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\ \color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{with $J\cup K=I$.}

We observe that

•

if
i\in J\backslash K
then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,

•

if
i\in K\backslash J
then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and

•

if
i\in J\cap K
then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

Also, by construction,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
, with
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
for
j\in J\backslash K
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})
for
j\in J\cap K
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
for
k\in K\backslash J
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})
for
k\in J\cap K
. We use the notation
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

It is immediate, by construction and induction hypothesis, to prove that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. Let us consider
E^{\prime}
, such that
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E^{\prime}
, namely
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j\in J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{k\in K}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
; then

(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j\in J\backslash K}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{k\in K\backslash J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in K\cap J}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}))

by associativity and commutativity of merge. We conclude by construction of
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and applying induction hypothesis. Now, if
E^{\prime}
is such that
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E^{\prime}
, we have
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}{}_{j}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}{}_{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We conclude by definition of
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
(in particular its behaviour with respect to receive constructs) and induction hypothesis.

[
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E
]
We have (hypothesis
(c)
)
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, namely

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\ \color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{.}

We observe that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and also that, by construction,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
, with
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})
. We use the notation

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

It is immediate, by construction and induction hypothesis, to prove that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. Let us consider
E^{\prime}
, such that
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E^{\prime}
, namely
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
; then

(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})

by associativity and commutativity of merge. We conclude by construction of
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and applying induction hypothesis. Now, if
E^{\prime}
is such that
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E^{\prime}
, we have
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We conclude by definition of
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
(in particular its behaviour with respect to send constructs) and induction hypothesis.

[
\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cap E=\emptyset
] We have
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. In this case

\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\\
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})=\\
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\end{array}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmLP}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}]_{i\in I}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}=([\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}])_{i\in I}
; we set
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})
. Thanks to Lemma
B.9
, we have that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
,
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We can then apply induction hypothesis to
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and have that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
, for all
i\in I
and for
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
. Let us consider now
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
this is equal to:

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

thanks to what obtained from Lemma
B.9
(see above). Analogously for
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. If
\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cap E^{\prime}\neq\emptyset
we have that the projections of
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
have the same message prefix
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, or
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
; are one analogous to the other. Let us consider the first one:

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

We conclude thanks to what obtained from induction hypothesis (see above). If
\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cap E^{\prime}=\emptyset
then

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\left(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\right)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\left(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\right)

and we conclude thanks to what obtained from induction hypothesis (see above) and by associativity and commutativity of merge.

Now, to have the full lemma for
t=1,\dots,n
, for a generic
n
, it is sufficient to proceed by induction on
n
: we observe that the bas cases are obvious and the binary case is above. We are left with
n\geq 3
, namely:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}

with
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{t=2,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}})

and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{t=2,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}})
. Since we have proved the theorem for the binary case and since
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{t=2,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
, we have that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{t=2,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
. In particular
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{t=2,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
allows us to apply induction hypothesis and we obtain, for

\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{t=2,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}})=[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]
, that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and that
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=2,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
for
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
, and thus we conclude.

∎

The
binary unmerge for projection

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf unmP^{bin}}}
takes a type
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
(first argument) and “unmerges” it, according to the structure of two other types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
(second and third arguments); thus
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf unmP^{bin}}}
returns a pair of types, the unmerged components of
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. The non-binary version of the
unmerge for projection

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf unmP}}
is defined by iterating the binary one, thus allowing for unmerging into
n
types. The role of this function is clarified by Lemma
B.15
.

To correctly define such function we need an auxiliary function that handles the case in which a localiser merge is equal to a projection one:
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j\in J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. In this case we want to obtain, for all
i\in I,j\in J
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{i}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
such that
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{i}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{i}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{i}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

Definition B.12 (Projection Unmerge for Localiser Merge).

The partial function
binary projection-unmerge for localiser merge

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}
is recursively defined by the following equations, whenever the recursive call is defined (we use
\pi_{1}
and
\pi_{2}
to indicate standard pair projections):

\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=((\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}),(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=((\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}),(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=((\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}),(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{where}\ \color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{n}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\pi_{n}(\pi_{m}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})))\ \text{for $n,m=1,2$}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}{}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}=\\
\hskip 18.49988pt((\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{1}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{2}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}),(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{1}}{}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{2}}{}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{where $J\cup K=I$ and, for $n,m=1,2$,}\ \color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{n}_{m}}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\begin{cases}\pi_{n}(\pi_{m}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}))\ \text{if $i\in J\cup K$}\\

The partial function
projection-unmerge for localiser merge

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;L_{L}\;L_{P}}}
takes as input two lists of types instead of two pairs. It returns a tuple of lists: if
L_{L}=[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]
and
L_{P}=[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]
the result list is of the kind
([\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{1}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{1}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}],\dots,[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{n}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}^{n}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}])
(
\pi_{i}
indicate the standard projection of the
i
-th component of the tuple).
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf unmPL}}
is defined by recursion (on the sum of the lengths of
L_{L}
and
L_{P}
) by the following equations.

\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[]\;L_{P}}}=([])\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]\;L_{P}}}=([L_{P}])\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]\;L_{P}}}=\begin{cases}([],[])\ \text{if $L_{P}=[]$}\\
([\pi_{1}(\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})),\pi_{1}(\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}))])\ \text{if $L_{P}=[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]$}\\
([\pi_{1}(\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})),\pi_{2}(\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}))],\\
\ \ [\pi_{1}(\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})),\pi_{2}(\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}))])\ \text{if $L_{P}=[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]$}\\
(\pi_{1}(\pi_{1}(U))\#L^{1},\pi_{1}(\pi_{2}(U))\#L^{2})\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{if $L_{P}=[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]$, where, for $i=1,2$}\\
\hskip 18.49988ptL^{i}=\pi_{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[(\pi_{2}(\pi_{1}(U))),(\pi_{2}(\pi_{2}(U)))]\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}})\ \text{and}\\
\hskip 18.49988ptU={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{k=2\dots m}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}\end{cases}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]\;L_{P}}}=(L^{1},\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]\;L^{2}}}),\dots,\pi_{n-1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]\;L^{2}}}))\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{where, for $i=1,2$,}\ L^{i}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{k=2,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})]\;L_{L}}}\end{array}

Lemma B.13.

We are given hybrid types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
such that
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{i=1,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j=1,\dots,m}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

Then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}][\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]
is defined and, if we set

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL}\;[\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}][\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]=([\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{1}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}],\dots,[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{n}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{n}_{m}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}])\text{,}

we have that:

•

\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j=1,\dots,m},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{i}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, for all
i=1,\dots,n
, and

•

\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{i=1,\dots,n},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}^{i}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, for all
j=1,\dots,n
.

Proof.

•

We first prove the base cases, in particular the case in which
n=2
and
m=2
(formally we first prove the result for
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf unmPL^{bin}}}
). This is done by structural induction on
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{L}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, exploiting the hypothesis and the inductive nature both of
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
and
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}
.

•

We conclude by induction on
n+m
, by following the definition of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf unmPL}}
.

∎

We now define the unmerge for the projection (Definition
B.10
) and ensure that it has the desired behaviour (Lemma
B.15
).

Definition B.14 (Unmerge for Projection).

The partial function
binary unmerge for projection

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}
is defined by the following equations, by recursion on
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})

\pi_{1}
and
\pi_{2}
to indicate standard pair projections):

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP^{bin}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{cases}\hskip-4.83694pt\begin{array}[]{l}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmPL^{bin}}\;[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}]_{i\in I}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]\;=\;(}}[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}])_{i\in I}$}\end{array}\end{cases}\end{array}

The partial function
unmerge for projection

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}
is defined by the following equations, by recursion on the list of hybrid types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, whenever the recursive call is defined:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[]}}=\text{undefined}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}=[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}=[\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}),\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})]\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))}}=\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\pi_{1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}({\sf set}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))}})\#{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\pi_{2}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP^{bin}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}({\sf set}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}))}})\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\#\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{L}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}\end{array}

Lemma B.15 (Unmerge for Projection).

We fix a set of participants
E
, and we are given hybrid types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
t=1,\dots,n
, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
such that:

(a)

{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\subseteq E
, for all
t=n
,

(b)

\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E=\emptyset
,

(c)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
;

then, for all
t=1,\dots,n
, we call
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
the
t
-th element of the list
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\dots,\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}}{}_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}]}}
and we have

(1)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger P}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, for all
t=1,\dots,n
and

(2)

\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t\in T}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

Proof.

As for Lemma
B.11
, the proof is pretty technical: we first prove the binary case
n=2
—which goes by induction on the
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
and needs lemma
B.13
—and then we generalise it to any
n
by induction. The proof is totally analogous to the proof of Lemma
B.11
, with the difference that localiser and projection “play inverted roles”.
∎

We give an algorithm to explicitly build “back” a hybrid type, from two compatible types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We first describe the algorithm as a (partial) recursive function in the next Definition
B.16
, then, with Theorem
B.17
, we prove that such functions has the desired behaviour.

Definition B.16 (Build-Back of a Single Component).

Given a set of participants
E
, the
build-back of a single component
is the partial recursive function
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}
defined below, by recursion on
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
. (In what follows we indicate by
\pi_{t}
the function that given a list returns its
t
-th element, when it exists.)

\begin{array}[]{l}\text{If ${\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\nsubseteq E$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}$ is undefined, otherwise it is defined by the following equations:}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1})|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E=\emptyset$ and both $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$ and $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$ are closed}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{if ${\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E=\emptyset$, $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$,}\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{and one of $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$ and $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$ is not closed}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{E}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E\neq\emptyset$ and $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{E}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$}\end{cases}\\
