{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E=\emptyset$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if ${\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E\neq\emptyset$ and ${\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E=\emptyset$}\end{cases}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{m_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}^{\prime}_{j}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;{\sf UL}_{j}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{with ${\sf UL}_{j}=\pi_{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}]_{j\in J}}})$, if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{m_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}^{\prime}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$ and ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{l_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;{\sf UP}_{i}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{with ${\sf UP}_{i}=\pi_{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}]_{i\in I}}})$, if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E$}\end{cases}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{m_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}^{\prime}_{j}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;{\sf UL}_{j}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{with ${\sf UL}_{j}=\pi_{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}]_{j\in J}}})$, if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{m_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}^{\prime}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$ and ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{l_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;{\sf UP}_{i}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{with ${\sf UP}_{i}=\pi_{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}]_{i\in I}}})$, if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E$}\end{cases}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{cases}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \text{if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$ or $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{m_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}^{\prime}_{j}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;{\sf UL}_{j}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{with ${\sf UL}_{j}=\pi_{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}]_{j\in J}}})$,}\\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{if $\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{m_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}^{\prime}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}$ and ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E$ or ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E$}\\
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{l_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;{\sf UP}_{i}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ \\
\hskip 18.49988pt\text{with ${\sf UP}_{i}=\pi_{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}]_{i\in I}}})$, if $\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cap E=\emptyset$}\end{cases}\\
\text{and undefined if none of the above applies.}\end{array}

We observe that, in the above definition, for
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
, we have left undefined the case in which
\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\subseteq E
. That is because
E
is the set of participants for a certain component and the messages
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
are for internal communication within this component. Namely such messages belong to
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, not to
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, which instead is the type for inter-component communication.

Finally, we prove the technical result that supports our whole theory. This theorem allows us to compose two hybrid types—
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, disciplining the inter-component communication of the system, and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, taking care of interactions within a single component—into one that retains all the information of the two, in terms of projections. The proof of this theorem relies on the just-proved lemmas and goes by a combination of induction on the depth of both given types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and of case analysis on these.

Theorem B.17 (Building Back a Single Component).

We fix a set of participants
E
, and we are given hybrid types
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, such that:

(a)

{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\subseteq E
,

(b)

\textsf{epart}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E=\emptyset
, and

(c)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
;

we set
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}
and we have:

(1)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and

(2)

for all
E^{\prime}
, such that
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
.

Moreover if
isGlobal(
H^{\dagger}
)
then
isGlobal(
H
)
.

Proof.

By induction on
d=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
. The case
d=0
is trivial; let us then have
d=n+1
and assume the theorem true for all
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
as in hypothesis, for which
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\leq n
. We proceed by case analysis on
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We call
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}
.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
]
By definition
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
; we observe that
\mathbf{1.}
projection onto a set containing all the internal participants is the identity function (Lemma
B.4
), and
\mathbf{2.}
projection of a closed type (
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is closed since its localisation is, Lemma
B.3
), onto a set of roles disjoint from the internal participants, is
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
(Lemma
B.5
).

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] We observe that, since
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, either
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, for which our proof is trivial, or
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, in which case we observe that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. By induction on
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sf{depth}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})
we prove our result.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{H^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] All cases go by induction hypothesis, but the second case, where
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
share some participant, but they are not both closed. In fact, both are not for condition
(c)
. Thanks to condition
(c)
, Lemma
B.5
, and a similar argument to the one used in the [
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] case above, we conclude by induction hypothesis.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] We observe that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
, (or
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
) without loss of generality. We conclude by induction hypothesis.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
]

(a)

If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
we conclude by construction of
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}
and induction hypothesis.

(b)

If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{m_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}^{\prime}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}).(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup^{L}}_{j\in J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We can therefore apply Lemma
B.11
and obtain that for
{\sf UL}_{j}=\pi_{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmL}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}]_{j\in J}}})
we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}={\sf UL}_{j}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
(for all
j\in J
) and
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j\in J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\sf UL}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E^{\prime}}}
(for all
E^{\prime}\cap E=\emptyset
). By construction (namely
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{m_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}^{\prime}_{j}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;{\sf UL}_{j}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}})\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
) and induction hypothesis we conclude.

(c)

If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E
, we have that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})
. We apply Lemma
B.15
and, for
{\sf UP}_{i}=\pi_{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf unmP}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;[\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}]_{i\in I}}})
, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\sf{loc}}\;}}{{\sf UP}_{i}}=(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})
and
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}{{\sf UP}_{i}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. By construction (namely
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}\;\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\;{\sf UP}_{i}}})\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
) and induction hypothesis we conclude.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] and [
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\dagger}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] These are analogous to to the above “send” case: in essence, they follow the different cases of the Definition
4.1
of
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathcal{B}^{1}_{E}}
which are similar for all three different kind of messages.

∎

B.2.
Projection for Hybrid Types and Set Inclusion

The following lemma is needed for proving preservation of projection (Theorem
B.19
below), which is essential for achieving distributed specification for communication protocols.

Lemma B.18 (Projection Distributes over Merge).

Given
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{n=1,\dots N}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}}

Proof.

Let us first prove the lemma for
n=2
:
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We proceed by induction on
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. Cases
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
are trivial.

Let us consider
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}{}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}{}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, with
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}{}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}{}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. If
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is closed and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is not guarded, than neither are
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}{}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}{}^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
(by reasoning on the participants of the result of
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
and the well-formedness of initial types), thus we conclude. In the other case, the thesis follows by induction hypothesis.

If
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{a}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{b}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
the thesis follow simply by applying induction hypothesis.

Let us consider the send case
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and hence
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E
then we conclude by definition of merge and induction hypothesis. If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E
, then

\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\overset{IH}{=}\\
\overset{IH}{=}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}((\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})))=\\
=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}\end{array}

We have marked with
\overset{IH}{=}
where we have exploited the induction hypothesis; all the other equalities follow by definitions or by associativity and commutativity of merge.

If
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, we have
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{j}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{j}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}{}_{j}\}_{j\in J}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{k}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{k}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}{}_{k}\}_{k\in K}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. Since,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, we observe that:

•

if
i\in J\backslash K
then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,

•

if
i\in K\backslash J
then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, and

•

if
i\in J\cap K
then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E
we conclude by applying the definition of projection to
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and induction hypothesis when
i\in J\cap K
. If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E
) we have

\begin{array}[]{l}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\\
=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in J\backslash K}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in K\backslash J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in J\cap K}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})=\\
=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j\in J\backslash K}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{k\in K\backslash J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in J\cap K}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\overset{IH}{=}\\
\overset{IH}{=}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j\in J\backslash K}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{k\in K\backslash J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in J\cap K}((\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})))=\\
=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{j\in J}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{1}_{j}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{k\in K}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{2}_{k}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}\end{array}

As above induction hypothesis, definition, and associativity and communtativity of merge.

The case
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{\texttt{\small S}}}_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
has four cases:

•

\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\subseteq E
, analogous to the send case above
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
, when
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}inE
.

•

{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E
, analogous to the send case above
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
, when
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}inE
.

•

{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E
, analogous to the receive case above
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
, when
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}inE
.

•

\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cap E=\emptyset
, analogous to the send case above
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\dots
, when
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E
.

Now, to have the full lemma for
t=1,\dots,n
, for a generic
n
, it is sufficient to proceed by induction on
n
: we observe that
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n-1}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. Now, thanks to the case
n=2
above, we have
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=((\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n-1}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n-1}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{n}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
. Since, by induction hypothesis
(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n-1}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{t=1,\dots,n-1}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{t}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
, we conclude.

∎

The following theorem proves that generalised projection on hybrid types is sound with respect to set inclusion. This is the conclusive step of our theory: after composing subprotocols into a general global type, thanks to the theorem below, we are guaranteed that, by projecting a hybrid type for some subprotocol onto one of its participant, we obtain
the same local type
as we would if we projected directly from the global type that we have obtained by composition, and that disciplines the whole communication.

Theorem B.19 (Projection Composes over Set Inclusion).

Given
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
E_{1}
and
E_{2}
, with
E_{2}\subseteq E_{1}
, assuming
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}
is defined,

(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}

Proof of Theorem
4.9
.

By induction on
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] and [
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] are immediate.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu X.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] We observe that, if
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is not guarded, then neither is
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, since
E_{2}\subseteq E_{1}
(this result is obtained simply by structural induction over
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
). Also, we know that the property of being closed is preserved by projection. In case
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is guarded, but
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mu\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{X}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}.\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is not, then (since
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}
is guarded, by Lemma
B.7
) we have that
{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\cap E_{2}=\emptyset
and hence also
{\textsf{ipart}}({(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}})})\cap E_{2}=\emptyset
(
{\textsf{ipart}}({(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}})})\subseteq{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})
; by structural induction on
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
). We can then conclude by following the defining rule for projection, in the recursion case (
[proj-rec]
in Definition
3.6
), and by applying induction hypothesis and Lemma
B.5
(depending on the cases).

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}|\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{2}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] Since projections are defined, w.l.o.g., we can assume
E_{2}\subseteq E_{1}\subseteq{\textsf{ipart}}({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})
. We conclude by direct application of induction hypothesis on
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ext}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] We observe that
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ext}}}}\notin E_{1}
.

(a)
If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\notin E_{1}
, then
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}{(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}
; by applying Lemma
B.18
and induction hypothesis we have
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}})
which is exactly what we need (since
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\notin E_{1}
entails
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\notin E_{2}
).

(b)
If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\in E_{1}\backslash E_{2}
, then
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ext}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. By induction hypothesis, we then have
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}
and we conclude.

(c)
If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\in E_{2}
, no merge happens and we conclude by simply unfolding the definition of projection and applying induction hypothesis.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ext}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] Analogous to the previous “send” case.

[
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
]

(a)
If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\notin E_{1}
, then
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\bigsqcup}_{i\in I}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}
. We conclude analogously to
(a)
in case [
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ext}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] above, by applying Lemma
B.18
and induction hypothesis.

(b)
If 1.
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\in E_{1}\setminus E_{2}
, 2.
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\in E_{1}\setminus E_{2}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\notin E_{1}
, or 3.
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\in E_{1}\setminus E_{2}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\notin E_{1}
, then we have, respectively:

(1)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,

(2)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, or

(3)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

In all cases, we conclude analogously to
(b)
in case [
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ext}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] above.

(c)
If 1.
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\in E_{2}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\notin E_{1}
, or 2.
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\in E_{2}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\notin E_{1}
, then we have, respectively:

(1)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, or

(2)

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

In both cases, we conclude analogously to
(c)
in case [
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{ext}}}}};\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5469}{S_{i}}}).\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
] above.

(d)
If 1.
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\in E_{2}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\in E_{1}\setminus E_{2}
, or 2.
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\in E_{2}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\in E_{1}\setminus E_{2}
, then we have, in both cases,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. Then, projecting on
E_{2}
, respectively:

(1)

(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}!{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, or

(2)

(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}};}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

In both cases, we conclude by induction hypothesis.

(e)
If
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\in E_{2}
then we have:
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

Then,
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}=\allowbreak\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}\to{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}:}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\{{{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}}(S_{i}).(}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{1}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E_{2}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{)\}_{i\in I}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
. We conclude by induction hypothesis.

∎

B.3.
Projection and Localiser for Hybrid Types with Delegation

We define here
projection
and
localiser

for our extension of hybrid types to delegation and
explicit connections, from §
5.4
.

Definition B.20 (Projection for Hybrid Types with Delegation).

The projection on the set of participants
E

for hybrid types with delegation, is a partial
operator, recursively
defined by the clauses in Figure
10
(whenever the recursive call is defined).

Merging
(
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
) is defined as a partial commutative operator
over two hybrid types such that: for all
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, it delves inductively inside all constructors (see Definition
3.6
), and

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bigwedge_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{p}}}}}}}_{i}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{?^{\star_{i}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{q}}}}}}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{?^{\star}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})&=&(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bigwedge_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{p}}}}}}}_{i}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{?^{\star_{i}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{q}}}}}}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\vee(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{?^{\star}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\\
&&\hskip 18.49988pt\text{if the resulting intersection is non-ambiguous}\\
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bigwedge_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{p}}}}}}}_{i}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{?^{\star_{i}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{q}}}}}}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{?^{\star}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})&=&(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bigwedge_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{p}}}}}}}_{i}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{?^{\star_{i}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{q}}}}}}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\\
&&\hskip 18.49988pt\text{if ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}={{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}_{i}$, ${{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}={{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}_{i}$, and $\ell=\ell_{i}$, for some $i\in I$}\\
(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bigwedge_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{p}}}}}}}_{i}?^{e}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{q}}}}}}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\mathtt{end}}&=&(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bigwedge_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{p}}}}}}}_{i}?^{e}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{q}}}}}}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\end{array}

We use an inductive version of merge,
which is more restrictive than (and hence compatible with)
the equirecursive merge in
(
Castellani et al. 2020
)
,
Definition
4.8
and following discussion. An input intersection
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bigwedge_{i\in I}\pi^{\textsf{in}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}{}_{i}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is
non-ambiguous

if all inputs are distinct and if
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}
,
whenever the intersection combines
a simple input
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}?{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
and connecting input
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}}}?^{e}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

The partial function

\_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{d}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathit{dE},E}}
(defined by the rules below)
generalises the delegation projection functions

\_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{1}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}})}}
and
\_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{2}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}})}}
from
(
Castellani et al. 2020
)

(Figure
6
); as in that paper, this function
is of a sequential nature: it is not defined
for branching, recursion, and parallel constructs.
In Figure
11
,
we provide the full definition.
To obtain well-delegated hybrid types
(see Definition
4.11
in
(
Castellani et al. 2020
)
)
and correctly nest delegation,
we have added the set argument
\mathit{dE}
, that keeps track
of the delegation pairs. We ask that,
if
\{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}),({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}})\}\subseteq\mathit{dE}
,
then
\{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}})\}\cap\{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}})\}=\emptyset
.
We can use one funtion
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{d}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{,}}
,
for generalising both
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{1}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{(,)}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{2}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{(,)}}
,
since we rely on the set
E
, namely:

\_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{1}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}})}}
is generalised by
\_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{d}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}})\},E}}
,
when
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\notin E
, while

\_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{2}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}})}}
is generalised by
\_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{d}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\{({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}})\},E}}

when
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\in E
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\notin E

In Figure
11
,
we use the following notation:

•

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\sigma}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
, for the projection extended to prefixes
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\sigma}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
, in the obvious way;

•

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{d}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\emptyset,E}}
, for
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}
;

•

\texttt{disj\_pair}\;({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}})\;\mathit{dE}
, when the following holds: for all
({{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}})\in\mathit{dE}
,

\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\}\cap\{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{s}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{r}}}}\}=\emptyset
.

The intuition behind these rules is
the following:
whenever a forward delegation is
encountered while projecting, the control is passed to

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{d}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{,}}
(e.g.,

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\circ\langle\!\langle\bullet}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}};\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\circ\langle\!\langle\bullet}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}[{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}];\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{d}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\{(p,q)\},E}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

if
{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}\in E
), which takes care
of projecting the sequence (single branch) in
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
of
elementary interactions (messages or
well-nested delegations, see
(
Castellani et al. 2020
)
,
Definition 4.11), until a dual backward delegation

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bullet\rangle\!\rangle\circ}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}};\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
is encountered and the control is
passed back to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}
, e.g.,

\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bullet\rangle\!\rangle\circ}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}};\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright^{d}}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\{(p,q)\},E}}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{[{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}]\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\bullet\rangle\!\rangle\circ}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}};\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H^{\prime}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\upharpoonright}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{E}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
.

Definition B.21 (Localiser for Hybrid Types with Delegation).

The localiser for hybrid types with delegation,
is a partial operator, recursively defined by the clauses in
Figure
12
(whenever the recursive call is defined).

Merging for the localiser
(
\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup}
) is defined as a partial
commutative operator
over two hybrid types such that:
for all
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}
,
it delves inductively inside all constructors
(see Definition
3.8
), and

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\boxplus_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{p}}}}}}}_{i}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{!^{\star}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{q}}}}}}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\sqcup^{L}}(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{?^{\star}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})&=&(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\boxplus_{i\in I}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{p}}}}}}}_{i}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{!^{\star}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}{{q}}}}}}}_{i}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell_{i}}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H_{i}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\boxplus(\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{p}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{!^{\star}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{{{\color[rgb]{0.0039,0.4727,0.4336}\textbf{{q}}}}}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}({{\color[rgb]{0.2813,0.2383,0.5508}{\ell}}});\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}{H}\color[rgb]{0.1,0.4,0.1}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0})\\

Delegation Projection for Hybrid Types with Delegation
